        <tr class="d-block">
    <td
      class="d-block color-fg-default comment-body markdown-body js-comment-body"
      
    >
        <p dir="auto">Thanks for the detailed report, <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/lcoronad/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/lcoronad">@lcoronad</a>! Yes, it may have to do with registration for reflection but it might also be something else in this specific case. As you pointed out, the <code class="notranslate">*Extensibility</code> classes are not a part of CXF. IIRC, CXF generates them dynamically on application start. We have some quirks in place in Quarkus CXF to replace them with our own static classes so that it works in native mode. We may have screwed something there. It would be great to have some minimal reproducer. There is probably something special in your WSDL that triggers those Extensors. You may post the reproducer here or via Red Hat support if you are a Red Hat customer and you would not like to share your code/WSDL publicly.</p>
    </td>
  </tr>

<p dir="auto">application.properties</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="######## Start Quarkus properties ###################

quarkus.log.console.format=%d{dd/MM/yyyy} %d{HH:mm:ss} %-5p %X{trnID} %c (%t) %s%e%n
service.logging.level=INFO
quarkus.log.category.&quot;com.cliente&quot;.level=${service.logging.level}
%dev.quarkus.log.category.&quot;com.cliente&quot;.level=DEBUG
%dev.quarkus.http.port=8080
quarkus.http.non-application-root-path=/q
quarkus.http.root-path=/api
quarkus.native.add-all-charsets=true
quarkus.hibernate-validator.enable=true

######## End Quarkus properties ###################

######## Start Soap Config ###################

quarkus.cxf.codegen.wsdl2java.includes=wsdl/*.wsdl
quarkus.cxf.client.consultarRestringido.wsdl=wsdl/ClientesService.wsdl
quarkus.cxf.client.consultarRestringido.client-endpoint-url=${WebServiceURL}

quarkus.cxf.endpoint.consultarRestringido.connection-timeout=${TimeOut}
quarkus.cxf.endpoint.consultarRestringido.receive-timeout=${TimeOut}

quarkus.cxf.codegen.wsdl2java.extra-args=-noAddressBinding,-autoNameResolution,-exsh=true,-dns=false,-p,com.cliente.services.clientes
quarkus.cxf.codegen.wsdl2java.binding-files=wsdl-bindings.xml

quarkus.jaxb.disable-test-namespace-schema=true
######## End Soap Config #######

######## Start Open API Configuration #######

quarkus.smallrye-openapi.path=/api/v1/openapi
quarkus.smallrye-openapi.info-title=Autenticacion comprobantes
quarkus.smallrye-openapi.info-version=1.0.0
quarkus.smallrye-openapi.info-description=Servicio para verificar si un cliente se encuentra en la lista de restringidos.
quarkus.smallrye-openapi.info-contact-email=c-djruiz@cliente.com
quarkus.smallrye-openapi.info-contact-name=Domingo Ruiz
quarkus.smallrye-openapi.info-license-name=Apache 2.0
quarkus.smallrye-openapi.info-license-url=http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0.html

######## End Open API Configuration #######

####### Start health check Configuration #######
quarkus.smallrye-health.root-path=/api/v1/health
######## End health check Configuration #######

####### Start Metrics Prometheus Configuration #######
quarkus.micrometer.export.prometheus.path=/metrics
####### End Metrics Prometheus Configuration #######

######## Start Swagger UI Configuration #######

quarkus.swagger-ui.always-include=true

######## End Swagger UI Configuration #######

######## Start Jacoco Configuration  #######

#Jacoco config - eliminamos paquetes de contenidos estaticos del reporte jacoco
quarkus.jacoco.excludes=com/cliente/exceptions/**,com/cliente/utils/**,**/com/brrd/service/services/*.class, com/cliente/dtos/inbound/*, com/cliente/intranet/services/*

# com/cliente/dtos/**,com/cliente/utils/**,

######## End Jacoco Configuration  #######

######## Start Dev OIDC Configuration Development#######
%dev.quarkus.oidc.client-id=backend-services
%dev.quarkus.oidc.credentials.secret=secret
%dev.quarkus.oidc.tls.verification=none
%dev.quarkus.oidc.enabled=true
######## End Dev OIDC Configuration Development#######

######## Start Dev Enable Policy Enforcement Development and Test#######

%dev.quarkus.keycloak.policy-enforcer.enable=false
%dev.quarkus.keycloak.devservices.port=9090
%dev.quarkus.keycloak.devservices.users.admin=admin
%dev.quarkus.keycloak.devservices.roles.admin=admin,user,consulta-restringido

######## End Dev Enable Policy Enforcement Development and Test#######

######## Start Dev OIDC Configuration Production#######
%prod.quarkus.oidc.auth-server-url=
%prod.quarkus.oidc.client-id=
%prod.quarkus.oidc.credentials.secret=
%prod.quarkus.oidc.tls.verification=none
%prod.quarkus.oidc.enabled=true
%prod.quarkus.oidc.authentication.authentication.add-openid-scope=false
######## End Dev OIDC Configuration Production#######

######## Start Enable Policy Enforcement Production#######

%prod.quarkus.oidc.keycloak.policy-enforcer.enable=false

######## End Enable Policy Enforcement Production#######"><pre class="notranslate"><code class="notranslate">######## Start Quarkus properties ###################

            <a width="fit" data-hydro-click="{&quot;event_type&quot;:&quot;discussions.click&quot;,&quot;payload&quot;:{&quot;event_context&quot;:&quot;DISCUSSION_VIEW&quot;,&quot;target&quot;:&quot;USER_PROFILE_LINK&quot;,&quot;current_repository_id&quot;:308083529,&quot;discussion_repository_id&quot;:308083529,&quot;org_level&quot;:false,&quot;discussion_id&quot;:8702834,&quot;discussion_comment_id&quot;:14189640,&quot;originating_url&quot;:&quot;https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/discussions/1882&quot;,&quot;user_id&quot;:null}}" data-hydro-click-hmac="ea8782204131b43cc1cabbbd51b98fac16bb46048582d7dd5056d9cc670a2486" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/ppalaga/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="/ppalaga" data-view-component="true" class="author Truncate-text lh-condensed Link--primary Link text-bold">ppalaga</a>

        <tr class="d-block">
    <td
      class="d-block color-fg-default comment-body markdown-body js-comment-body tmp-px-3 pt-0 pb-2"
      
    >
        <p dir="auto">Thanks again for the inputs, <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/lcoronad/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/lcoronad">@lcoronad</a>!</p>
<h2 dir="auto">Reproducible with Quarkus CXF 3.15</h2>
<p dir="auto">I was able to reproduce your console output with Quarkus 3.15. Here is my code: <a href="https://github.com/ppalaga/d1882-native-extensibility/tree/3.15">https://github.com/ppalaga/d1882-native-extensibility/tree/3.15</a><br>
One needs to run the native test using (on Linux)</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mvn clean verify -Pnative -Dquarkus.native.container-build"><pre class="notranslate"><code class="notranslate">mvn clean verify -Pnative -Dquarkus.native.container-build
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The output is then much like yours.</p>
<p dir="auto">I'd like to point out that the error stack traces logged at <code class="notranslate">DEBUG</code> level do not seem to be harmful. The tests in the reproducer app is passing anyway.</p>
<p dir="auto">To get to the state showing your error messages, I had to add <code class="notranslate">@RegisterForReflection(classNames = {"com.ibm.wsdl.extensions.http.HTTPBindingImpl", "com.ibm.wsdl.extensions.http.HTTPAddressImpl"})</code> around here: <a href="https://github.com/ppalaga/d1882-native-extensibility/blob/3.15/src/main/java/io/quarkiverse/cxf/reproducer/Rest.java#L13">https://github.com/ppalaga/d1882-native-extensibility/blob/3.15/src/main/java/io/quarkiverse/cxf/reproducer/Rest.java#L13</a><br>
That is a valid issue per se that still exists in the latest versions of Quarkus CXF.<br>
I created a separate tracker for it: <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3345353650" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/issues/1891" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/issues/1891/hovercard" href="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/issues/1891">#1891</a></p>
<h2 dir="auto">Not reproducible on Quarkus CXF 3.20+</h2>
<p dir="auto">When upgrading the reproducer app to 3.20 or 3.26 the exceptions are not logged anymore - see <a class="commit-link" data-hovercard-type="commit" data-hovercard-url="https://github.com/ppalaga/d1882-native-extensibility/commit/5f7ea8975b480f9e301467ab319257f9802289cd/hovercard" href="https://github.com/ppalaga/d1882-native-extensibility/commit/5f7ea8975b480f9e301467ab319257f9802289cd">ppalaga/d1882-native-extensibility@<tt>5f7ea89</tt></a> and <a class="commit-link" data-hovercard-type="commit" data-hovercard-url="https://github.com/ppalaga/d1882-native-extensibility/commit/1296e60a78138f7a387f58d1a7233b55128c8a9c/hovercard" href="https://github.com/ppalaga/d1882-native-extensibility/commit/1296e60a78138f7a387f58d1a7233b55128c8a9c">ppalaga/d1882-native-extensibility@<tt>1296e60</tt></a></p>
<h2 dir="auto">Possible solutions</h2>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Upgrade to a Quarkus CXF 3.20.x version, such as 3.20.2.SP1-redhat-00003 (if you are a Red Hat customer) or the latest 3.20.x community version or even newest community version 3.25.x or 3.26.x (expected next week).</li>
<li>If you cannot upgrade from 3.15, you may ask for a fix through the official Red Hat support channel</li>
</ul>
    </td>
  </tr>

    </tbody>
  </table>
